Census limits are uncalled off-map records, the dead map-49 service suffix and any real isolated self-loop safety limit. Destination admission remains separate from operation support. Darkside rows are outside this Clouds scope.

## Interpretation

Read-only World of Xeen CD census. Original resources are never exported. The report retains only counts, identifiers and in-game names.

Support comes from engine decoders and execution with original New Game owners or prepared Clouds developer Event-fixture owners, geometry, actors and Journey RNG (seed 7), initialized by XeenEncounterFlow. Each operation uses a fresh detached candidate; mutations never reach the retained source or disk. Clouds fixtures admit only their selected map; ordinary map-admission restrictions remain separate. Off-map subroutines use physical callers found in the resource. Original line numbers are retained, with inert suffixes at sequential and branch destinations; isolated true self-targets retain the 1024-dispatch safety limit and remain unclassified census limits. This is not a reachability or full-playthrough proof. Terminal Vertigo services are also checked through the live EventFlow handoff. The mainland legacy well uses live Flow publication. Original quest-counter refusals, proficiency and equipment conflicts are gameplay rules, not missing implementation. When the same mechanic is admitted elsewhere, its engine refusal is partial in that context instead of a globally missing mechanic. Audio remains partial because its original timing/animation runs silently. Records rejected as malformed and evidence not classifiable by engine checks remain unclassified.

M70 Clouds Event exclusions: audio (6/40/60), town services (17, beyond current Vertigo actions), and the ending (30 plus ConfirmWord ending keywords). Darkside-only operations and durable banks are outside Clouds scope. Destination-map admission is separate: shared travel refusals preserve candidate state and RNG. Residual census limits are off-map records without a discovered physical caller, real isolated self-loops (1024 dispatches), and mirror transfers without a local ConfirmWord prefix. No unresolved in-scope Clouds Event mechanic is accepted as an exclusion.

Items measure Equip/Use. Equipment combat/Rest is separate: it asks the actual xeenValidateJourneyMelee guard, which since M67 admits every material, effectiveness counter and broken/cursed state the original accepts and refuses only records outside the generated tables. No predicate from the old diagnostic monster profile is used to judge current scene animation. The calibration gate accepts only audio and six service-action gaps in Vertigo; mirror and password input run through the interpreter. Calibration exceptions: PlayFX, PlayEventVoc and PlayCD preserve presentation but lack audio; four Event row types (Return, Display0x01, If2 mode 44 and WhoWill) remain unclassified because their off-map subroutines have no physical caller in the resource. No missing owner, RNG, publication or service provider is an allowed Vertigo exception. Generic arrival effects, object/wall behavior, resistance and drop rows still carry explicit unknowns; they do not certify a map as playable.

Rows split when the same mechanic has different support reasons. Uses mean instruction records, monster placements (including disabled spawn slots), terrain cells, object/wall placements or explicit item references, as appropriate. Catalogue entries have zero uses unless referenced. Monster maps include encounter-table membership even when no placed actor uses a slot (zero additional placements). Map impact is the number of distinct maps containing a row; rows are sorted by unsupported/partial first, then map impact and occurrences. Map resources include the neighboring 16x16 geometry tiles used by larger indoor locations, not just primary map destinations. Random treasure/stock does not identify a particular generated item; its generation sites are reported separately. Shared spell/item catalogues appear on both sides. Darkside counts describe resources; Clouds-only engine admission remains a separate map blocker.

Monsters have no party-spell ID in the reference format: their magic is represented by attack type, special and range rows. No monster-to-party-spell crosswalk is inferred. Spell uses count book references and item powers, not casting frequency. Unclassified rows identify the missing evidence: arrival effects, object/wall behavior, resistances, generic monster drops and rejected resource candidates. No route simulation or random-drop expansion is performed.

## Screens — manual checklist

This is the one manual dimension, taken from the pinned ScummVM dialog set,
with the main screen, town locations and World/CD menu added from the same pin.
It is not inferred from original data and the guard cannot discover UI changes.
Update this checklist when a screen or button changes. A partial row names its
missing buttons or contexts. The list describes Clouds; Darkside has no gameplay
entry. Dialog identifiers below are reference identifiers, not commercial text.

| Reference dialog / screen | Status | Buttons and limitations | MMModern owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Awards | supported | Sheet member only; nine lines, Up/Down arrows and keypad 8/2, mouse buttons, Exit | XeenCharacterDialogFlow |
| CopyProtection | unsupported | Version-specific word input; not a World CD gameplay requirement | — |
| CreateCharacterDialog | unsupported | Roll, Create, portrait, stats, class, name, Exit | — |
| Dismiss | unsupported | Member choice, Exit | XeenEventFlow |
| PartyDialog | unsupported | Roster, recruit, Delete, Remove, Create, Exit | — |
| Quests | supported | Quest Items, Current Quests, Auto Notes; arrows/keypad, Page Up/Down, Home/End, mouse buttons, Exit; exploration and Items, including combat | XeenEventFlow |
| Choose123 / HowMuch | unsupported | General Event choice and amount input | XeenEventInterpreter |
| SelectElement | unsupported | Fire, Electricity, Cold, Acid, cancel | — |
| LloydsBeacon | unsupported | Set, Return | — |
| Teleport | unsupported | Distance, cancel | — |
| TownPortal | unsupported | Town choice, cancel | — |
| IdentifyMonster / DetectMonsters | unsupported | Monster information, acknowledge | — |
| Arena / PyramidLocation | unsupported | Arena choice; switch sides | — |
| Reaper / Golem / Dwarf / Sphinx locations | unsupported | Scripted location sequences | — |
| OtherOptions / endings | unsupported | Clouds/Darkside intros and endings; World ending; Escape works | XeenTitleFlow |
| Exploration main screen | partial | Movement, strafe, Shoot, Cast, Rest, Bash, Quests, Map, Info, Quick Ref, gem, portraits work; Dismiss refuses | XeenEventFlow |
| Combat main screen | partial | Attack, Block, Shoot effects, Cast, charged-item Use (including Town Portal), Quick Fight/options, targets, turns, Info, Quick Ref; Run outdoors only | XeenEncounterFlow |
| CharacterInfo | supported | Stats and popups, Items, Awards, Quick Ref, Exchange, Exit; original combat Exchange refusal | XeenCharacterDialogFlow |
| ControlPanel | partial | Save, Save As, Load, Quit work; Efx, Music, Speech, Text, Mr Wizard unavailable | XeenEventFlow |
| ItemsDialog | partial | Categories, Equip, Remove, Discard, transfer, Use, Quest, Exit; free combat equipment/Discard/transfer, combat-party recipients, main-screen Use; sheet Use refused in combat; Recharge/Enchant/Gold supported | XeenInventoryFlow |
| Input / StringInput / NumericInput | partial | Save names, Bank amounts work; general Event inputs unavailable | XeenTitleFlow / XeenEventFlow |
| SpellsDialog / CastSpell / SpellOnWho | partial | Select, Buy, Info, rows, scroll, Cast, New, member choice, cancel work; effects limited by Spells dimension | XeenCastingUi |
| Tavern | partial | Food, Drink, Tip, Rumors, member selection, Exit work; Sign In awaits the inn | XeenEventFlow |
| LocationMessage / NPC | partial | Acknowledge and Yes/No; other NPC modes unavailable | XeenEventPresenter |
| PleaseWait | unclassified | Reference helper; no separately identified equivalent screen | — |
| CreditsScreen | supported | Four original pages, acknowledge | XeenTitleFlow |
| DifficultyDialog | supported | Adventurer, Warrior | XeenTitleFlow |
| ExchangeDialog | supported | Member choice, Exit; original combat refusal | XeenCharacterDialogFlow |
| InfoDialog | supported | Date/time/effects, acknowledge | XeenCharacterDialogFlow |
| ItemSelectionDialog | supported | Rows, Exit for implemented item actions | XeenInventoryFlow |
| MapDialog | supported | Map, acknowledge | XeenEventFlow |
| MessageDialog / ErrorScroll / CantCast | supported | Message/refusal, acknowledge | XeenEventPresenter / XeenCastingUi |
| Confirm / YesNo | supported | Yes, No | XeenEventPresenter |
| QuickFight | supported | Next action, member, Exit; action effects retain combat restrictions | XeenEncounterFlow |
| QuickReferenceDialog | supported | Party summary, acknowledge | XeenCharacterDialogFlow |
| NotWhileEngaged | supported | Original combat refusal, acknowledge | XeenCastingUi |
| WhoWill | supported | Member choice, cancel | XeenEventPresenter |
| Bank | supported | Deposit, Withdraw, Gold, Gems, amount, Exit | XeenEventFlow |
| Temple | supported | Heal, Donation, Uncurse, member selection, Exit | XeenSmithFlow |
| Blacksmith | supported | Browse, Buy, Sell, Identify, Fix, Exit | XeenSmithFlow |
| Guild | supported | Buy Spells, Spell Info, member selection, Exit; Vertigo catalogue | XeenCastingUi |
| Training | supported | Train, member, Exit; Vertigo cap | XeenTrainingFlow |
| World/CD main menu | supported | Start, Load, Credits, Other Options | XeenTitleFlow |
| Save/load chooser | supported | Slots, names, overwrite/cancel; original DOS presentation | XeenTitleFlow |

Reference caveats: ScummVM merges Save As into Save and uses its generic save
chooser. MMModern retains the DOS Save As button and original chooser. The
reference's control panel omits the CD Speech/Text toggles, included above from
MMModern's DOS UI. Abstract ButtonContainer, UIButton and SettingsBaseDialog are
not independent screens.
